# ADR 0043 §"Refused: SMIL animation". `svg/animations/` is 280 of this area's 769
# Firefox-gap files and every one of them wants a second animation timeline beside CSS
# animations -- with its own event set and its own syncbase dependency graph -- plus a
# clock that a document starts on load and never stops, against a main loop whose whole
# design is that idle CPU is zero. Deliberate; do not "fix". The condition to revisit is
# in the ADR and is checkable: the Web Animations timeline exists, and one of ADR 0007's
# five compatibility targets is *measured* to use SMIL.

[svg/styling/attr-style-media.html]
FAIL=Setting media via the IDL attribute updates the content attribute
FAIL=Setting the content attribute updates the IDL attribute (media)
# This browser has one `SVGElement` interface for every SVG tag (see the tag
# table in src/bindings/NodeInterfaces.cpp), and `title` is an IDL attribute of
# `SVGStyleElement` in particular. These two passed until 2026-08-11 only
# because an SVG `<style>` was being given `HTMLStyleElement.prototype` -- the
# interface was chosen by tag name with no regard for the namespace, which also
# made `svgStyle instanceof HTMLStyleElement` true. Adding `title` to
# `SVGElement` to keep them green would put it on every SVG element, which is
# ADR 0012's stub by another route. Wants the SVG interface hierarchy.
